	Awakening after nightfall, you're acutely reminded of what a hangover felt like as a mortal. You swing your feet out from your ${bed} and stand, stretching out the muscles in your back with an uncomfortable snapping and popping. A quick examination of your wound shows significant mending while you were asleep, but there's a long way to go yet before full recovery.
	
	Gerard greets you from behind his desk after you leave the bedroom in search of your gun and anything else you might have had on you last night when you dragged yourself inside. "You're looking a fair bit better than you did last night if you don't mind my saying," he says. It sounds like he's being sincere, but it's hard to tell over the ringing in your ears. "I wish I had better news for you," he continues.
	
	You groan loudly. What else could go wrong?
	
	"While you were engaged in the sewers last night, there was an attack on Elysium. I've heard conflicting reports, but from what I can gather it sounds like the entire floor was incinerated."
	
	You're not sure you heard that right. "Incinerated?"
	
	"Yes, ${sirmaam}. I'm afraid so." He bites his lip. He's rattled—these successive nights of violence are taking their toll on his nerves. 
	
	You take another quick look at your wound and groan again. Louder this time.
	
	*goto gerard_fears

*comment Noted early in ch2, Bouchard collects and maintains weapons as a personal passion and link to his mortal life, and I very much feel like the below fits with him as a character. 
When Bouchard asked if you've fired a machine gun, you thought he may have meant a standard military-grade selective fire rifle switched to full-auto. You've been supplied with at least a hundred rounds of belt-fed ammo—more than enough to reduce even the eldest vampire into small chunks of meat and red mist. *achieve qui_to_his_heart
It was surprising how quickly the night fell away from you, dissolved into flickering snapshots of passion rising in intensity and inevitable as the sun itself. At first, you'd been startled by the warmth of his skin against yours, comforting and caressing as your own body slowly flushed with the blush of life, rousing the blood you'd so recently liberated to a new purpose. 

You run your hand down his back, feeling muscles tighten and relax, framed by the firebrand of his scar, pulsating with the rhythm of his simulated heartbeat. Losing yourself to the ecstasy of the flesh, you bring the tips of your fangs to his neck, pricking warm skin and tasting the sweet vitae beneath. Even with your senses reeling from the intense pleasure of the Kiss, it's impossible to miss his excitement as he wraps his arms tightly around you, pulling you closer as you savor an intimacy mortals may only dream of. 

You don't remember pulling away, but in the heady haze of euphoria you suddenly find your positions reversed, Qui's fangs piercing your own neck, beginning a circle of Blood-sharing that will bond you to each other both in body and spirit. Such bonds are perilous, often fraught with danger, but somehow the knowledge of its risk only makes it that much sweeter.

The first tendrils of light brush against the front porch, a precursor to the dawn, and within the confines of the well-shielded home passion slowly gives way to the lure of sleep. Ensnared in each other's arms, you grudgingly relent and fall back into the slumber of the dead, skin cooling, heaving breaths gone still, hearts stopped.
